<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72">  const</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0550AE;--shiki-dark:#79C0FF"> known</span><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72"> =</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3"> panels.</span><span style="--shiki-light:#8250DF;--shiki-dark:#D2A8FF">map</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">((</span><span style="--shiki-light:#953800;--shiki-dark:#FFA657">p</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">) </span><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72">=></span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3"> (p.defaultSize </span><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72">===</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0550AE;--shiki-dark:#79C0FF"> undefined</span><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72"> ?</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0550AE;--shiki-dark:#79C0FF"> null</span><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72"> :</span><span style="--shiki-light:#8250DF;--shiki-dark:#D2A8FF"> toPercent</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">(p.defaultSize, groupPx)))</span></span>

<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#6E7781;--shiki-dark:#8B949E">/** Round away float noise: a size like -3e-15 is an invalid flex-grow and would be ignored by the browser. */</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72">const</span><span style="--shiki-light:#8250DF;--shiki-dark:#D2A8FF"> clean</span><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72"> =</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3"> (</span><span style="--shiki-light:#953800;--shiki-dark:#FFA657">sizes</span><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72">:</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0550AE;--shiki-dark:#79C0FF"> number</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">[]) </span><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72">=></span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3"> sizes.</span><span style="--shiki-light:#8250DF;--shiki-dark:#D2A8FF">map</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">((</span><span style="--shiki-light:#953800;--shiki-dark:#FFA657">v</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">) </span><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72">=></span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3"> Math.</span><span style="--shiki-light:#8250DF;--shiki-dark:#D2A8FF">max</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">(</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0550AE;--shiki-dark:#79C0FF">0</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">, Math.</span><span style="--shiki-light:#8250DF;--shiki-dark:#D2A8FF">round</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">(v </span><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72">*</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0550AE;--shiki-dark:#79C0FF"> 1000</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">) </span><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72">/</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0550AE;--shiki-dark:#79C0FF"> 1000</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">))</span></span>

<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72">type</span><span style="--shiki-light:#953800;--shiki-dark:#FFA657"> ResizablePanelGroupProps</span><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72"> =</span><span style="--shiki-light:#953800;--shiki-dark:#FFA657"> Omit</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">&#x3C;</span><span style="--shiki-light:#953800;--shiki-dark:#FFA657">React</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">.</span><span style="--shiki-light:#953800;--shiki-dark:#FFA657">ComponentProps</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">&#x3C;</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0A3069;--shiki-dark:#A5D6FF">"div"</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">>, </span><span style="--shiki-light:#0A3069;--shiki-dark:#A5D6FF">"dir"</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">> </span><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72">&#x26;</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3"> {</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#953800;--shiki-dark:#FFA657">  direction</span><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72">?:</span><span style="--shiki-light:#953800;--shiki-dark:#FFA657"> ResizableDirection</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#6E7781;--shiki-dark:#8B949E">  /** Persists sizes and collapsed state in `localStorage["resizable:&#x3C;autoSaveId>"]`. */</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#953800;--shiki-dark:#FFA657">  autoSaveId</span><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72">?:</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0550AE;--shiki-dark:#79C0FF"> string</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#6E7781;--shiki-dark:#8B949E">  /** Called with the sizes (percent) whenever a drag, a key press or a collapse settles. */</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#8250DF;--shiki-dark:#D2A8FF">  onLayout</span><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72">?:</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3"> (</span><span style="--shiki-light:#953800;--shiki-dark:#FFA657">sizes</span><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72">:</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0550AE;--shiki-dark:#79C0FF"> number</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">[]) </span><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72">=></span><span style="--shiki-light:#0550AE;--shiki-dark:#79C0FF"> void</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#6E7781;--shiki-dark:#8B949E">  /** Imperative API: collapse / expand / toggle a panel by id, read or set sizes. */</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#953800;--shiki-dark:#FFA657">  handleRef</span><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72">?:</span><span style="--shiki-light:#953800;--shiki-dark:#FFA657"> React</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">.</span><span style="--shiki-light:#953800;--shiki-dark:#FFA657">Ref</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">&#x3C;</span><span style="--shiki-light:#953800;--shiki-dark:#FFA657">ResizablePanelGroupHandle</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">></span></span>

<span class="line"></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#6E7781;--shiki-dark:#8B949E">/**</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#6E7781;--shiki-dark:#8B949E"> * Split panes with draggable dividers. Panels size in percent of the group (or</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#6E7781;--shiki-dark:#8B949E"> * pixel strings for mins and defaults), resize live while dragging, collapse</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#6E7781;--shiki-dark:#8B949E"> * past their minimum when `collapsible`, and can persist their layout.</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#6E7781;--shiki-dark:#8B949E"> */</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72">function</span><span style="--shiki-light:#8250DF;--shiki-dark:#D2A8FF"> ResizablePanelGroup</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">({</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#953800;--shiki-dark:#FFA657">  direction</span><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72"> =</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0A3069;--shiki-dark:#A5D6FF"> "horizontal"</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">,</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#953800;--shiki-dark:#FFA657">  autoSaveId</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">,</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#953800;--shiki-dark:#FFA657">  onLayout</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">,</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#953800;--shiki-dark:#FFA657">  handleRef</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">,</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#953800;--shiki-dark:#FFA657">  className</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">,</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#953800;--shiki-dark:#FFA657">  children</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">,</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72">  ...</span><span style="--shiki-light:#953800;--shiki-dark:#FFA657">props</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">}</span><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72">:</span><span style="--shiki-light:#953800;--shiki-dark:#FFA657"> ResizablePanelGroupProps</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">) {</span></span>

<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72">type</span><span style="--shiki-light:#953800;--shiki-dark:#FFA657"> ResizablePanelProps</span><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72"> =</span><span style="--shiki-light:#953800;--shiki-dark:#FFA657"> React</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">.</span><span style="--shiki-light:#953800;--shiki-dark:#FFA657">ComponentProps</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">&#x3C;</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0A3069;--shiki-dark:#A5D6FF">"div"</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">> </span><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72">&#x26;</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3"> {</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#6E7781;--shiki-dark:#8B949E">  /** Stable id, used by the imperative API and for persisted layouts. */</span></span>

<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#6E7781;--shiki-dark:#8B949E">  /** Dragging well past `minSize` (or calling `collapse`) snaps the panel to `collapsedSize`. */</span></span>

<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72">function</span><span style="--shiki-light:#8250DF;--shiki-dark:#D2A8FF"> ResizablePanel</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">({ </span><span style="--shiki-light:#953800;--shiki-dark:#FFA657">id</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">, </span><span style="--shiki-light:#953800;--shiki-dark:#FFA657">className</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">, </span><span style="--shiki-light:#953800;--shiki-dark:#FFA657">style</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">, </span><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72">...</span><span style="--shiki-light:#953800;--shiki-dark:#FFA657">rest</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3"> }</span><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72">:</span><span style="--shiki-light:#953800;--shiki-dark:#FFA657"> ResizablePanelProps</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">) {</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#6E7781;--shiki-dark:#8B949E">  // Sizing props are read by the group from the element; keep them off the DOM node.</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72">  const</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0550AE;--shiki-dark:#79C0FF"> props</span><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72">:</span><span style="--shiki-light:#953800;--shiki-dark:#FFA657"> Record</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">&#x3C;</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0550AE;--shiki-dark:#79C0FF">string</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">, </span><span style="--shiki-light:#0550AE;--shiki-dark:#79C0FF">unknown</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">> </span><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72">=</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3"> { </span><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72">...</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">rest }</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72">  for</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3"> (</span><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72">const</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0550AE;--shiki-dark:#79C0FF"> key</span><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72"> of</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0550AE;--shiki-dark:#79C0FF"> PANEL_CONFIG_KEYS</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">) </span><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72">delete</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3"> props[key]</span></span>

<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72">  const</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0550AE;--shiki-dark:#79C0FF"> panelId</span><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72"> =</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3"> ctx.panels[index]?.id </span><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72">??</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3"> id</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72">  const</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0550AE;--shiki-dark:#79C0FF"> isCollapsed</span><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72"> =</span><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72"> !!</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">(panelId </span><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72">&#x26;&#x26;</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3"> ctx.collapsed[panelId])</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72">  return</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3"> (</span></span>
